The following is a '''list of [[album]]s, [[Extended play|EPs]], and [[mixtape]]s released in 2025'''. These albums are (1) original, i.e. excluding [[reissue]]s, [[remasters]], and [[Compilation album|compilations]] of previously released recordings, and (2) [[WP:MUS|notable]], defined as having received significant coverage from reliable sources independent of the subject.

For additional information about bands formed, reformed, disbanded, or on hiatus, for deaths of musicians, and for links to musical awards, see [[2025 in music]].
